## TDD evidence

Commands and observed RED/GREEN results copied from the Task 1-4 execution reports:

| Task | Focus | RED command and observed failure | GREEN command and observed count |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Boundaries | `..\p01\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest tests.test_data_audit.AuditBoundaryTests -v`: first `ModuleNotFoundError: tests.test_data_audit`, then intended `ModuleNotFoundError: tools`. | Same command: 5 tests, OK. |
| 1 fix 1 | Linked roots | Same boundary command: 9 tests with 2 failures, both `ValueError not raised`. | Same command: 9 tests, OK; full discovery suite 101 tests, OK. |
| 2 | Profiling | `..\p01\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest tests.test_data_audit.CsvProfilingTests -v`: import failure for `KeyRule`. | `..\p01\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest tests.test_data_audit -v`: 13 tests, OK. |
| 2 fix 1 | Empty/middle malformed files | Two named regression tests: 2 tests, one `StopIteration` error and one missing-`ValueError` failure. | Same two-test command: 2 tests, OK; audit suite 15, full suite 107. |
| 3 | Lineage | `..\p01\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest tests.test_data_audit.LineageTests -v`: import failure for `build_lineage`. | Same command: 3 tests, OK; audit suite 18. |
| 3 fix 1 | Evidence hardening | Same lineage command: 7 tests with 4 failures (two wrong statuses, untracked evidence included, duplicate backtest basename loss). | Same command: 7 tests, OK; audit suite 22; full suite 114. |
| 3 fix 2 | Deleted tracked source | Same lineage command: 8 tests with `FileNotFoundError` for deleted tracked Python. | Same command: 8 tests, OK; audit suite 23; full suite 115. |
| 4 | Rendering/CLI | `..\p01\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest tests.test_data_audit.AuditCommandTests -v`: import failure for `atomic_write`. | Same command: 3 tests, OK; audit suite 26; full suite 118. |
| 4 fix 1 | Contract/root evidence | Same command produced the expected missing CLI/discovery/summary assertions and silent non-Git-success failures. | Same command: 6 tests, OK; audit suite 29; full suite 121. |
| 4 fix 2 | Renderer API | Same command failed with `TypeError: render_inventory() missing 2 required positional arguments`. | Same command: 7 tests, OK; audit suite 30; full suite 122. |

The real audit exposed one additional evidence error: unknown CSVs were assigned a candidate `date` key even when their headers had no `date`, causing all 1,474 Eris cache artifacts to become `ProfileFailure` rows.

- RED: `..\p01\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest tests.test_data_audit.CsvProfilingTests.test_unknown_csv_without_date_is_profiled_without_a_candidate_key -v` -> 1 test, failure because the result was `ProfileFailure(... tuple.index(x): x not in tuple)`.
- GREEN: same command -> 1 test, OK.
- Focused GREEN: `..\p01\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest tests.test_data_audit.CsvProfilingTests -v` -> 7 tests, OK.
- Smallest fix: unconfigured CSVs use `KeyRule()`; only explicitly named artifacts receive candidate keys.

The first independent lineage review then exposed a second evidence error: all 518 real rows had zero `unused` classifications because the rule required a non-empty downstream-profile set, making terminal copied columns impossible to classify as unused.

- RED: `..\p01\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest tests.test_data_audit.LineageTests.test_terminal_copied_column_with_only_upstream_evidence_is_unused -v` -> 1 failure, observed `diagnostic` instead of `unused`.
- GREEN: same command -> 1 test, OK.
- Focused GREEN: `..\p01\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest tests.test_data_audit.LineageTests -v` -> 9 tests, OK.
- Audit GREEN: `..\p01\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest tests.test_data_audit -v` -> 32 tests, OK.
- Smallest fix: for a copied terminal backtest field, only an exact token in the terminal writer counts as use; upstream-only token evidence no longer masks a terminal unused field. Unknown units remain discrepancies.

The reviewer's exact hash command (exit 0) was:

```powershell
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$env:PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8'
@'
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,re
root=Path(r'C:\Users\jaydo_0v7vk2o\Downloads\Swap_Arbitrage')
inv=Path('docs/data/current-inventory.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
sections=re.split(r'(?m)^### `([^`]+)`\r?\n',inv)
doc={}
for i in range(1,len(sections),2):
    m=re.search(r'(?m)^- SHA-256: `([0-9a-f]{64})`$',sections[i+1])
    if m: doc[sections[i]]=m.group(1)
def sha(p):
    h=hashlib.sha256()
    with p.open('rb') as f:
        for b in iter(lambda:f.read(1024*1024),b''): h.update(b)
    return h.hexdigest()
files=sorted((root/'data').rglob('*.csv'),key=lambda p:p.relative_to(root).as_posix())
if (root/'r2_objects.csv').is_file(): files.append(root/'r2_objects.csv')
cache=[p for p in files if p.relative_to(root).as_posix().startswith('data/cache/')]
current=[p for p in files if p not in cache]
cache_current=[]; mismatches=[]; missing_doc=[]
for p in cache:
    rel=p.relative_to(root).as_posix(); actual=sha(p); cache_current.append((rel,actual))
    expected=doc.get(rel)
    if expected is None: missing_doc.append(rel)
    elif expected != actual: mismatches.append((rel,expected,actual))
def agg(items):
    h=hashlib.sha256()
    for rel,digest in items: h.update(f'{rel}\t{digest}\n'.encode())
    return h.hexdigest()
cache_doc=[(rel,doc[rel]) for rel,_ in cache_current if rel in doc]
print(f'CACHE_FILES={len(cache_current)}')
print(f'CACHE_CURRENT_AGGREGATE={agg(cache_current)}')
print(f'CACHE_DOCUMENTED_AGGREGATE={agg(cache_doc)}')
print(f'CACHE_DOC_MISSING={len(missing_doc)}')
print(f'CACHE_HASH_MISMATCHES={len(mismatches)}')
print(f'CURRENT_FILES={len(current)}')
for p in current:
    rel=p.relative_to(root).as_posix(); actual=sha(p); expected=doc.get(rel)
    print(f'CURRENT_HASH\t{rel}\t{actual}\tDOC={expected}\tMATCH={actual==expected}')
'@ | & '..\p01\.venv\Scripts\python.exe' -
```

The exact current-artifact profile command (exit 0) was:

```powershell
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$env:PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8'
@'
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter
import csv,re
root=Path(r'C:\Users\jaydo_0v7vk2o\Downloads\Swap_Arbitrage')
inv=Path('docs/data/current-inventory.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
parts=re.split(r'(?m)^### `([^`]+)`\r?\n',inv)
doc={parts[i]:parts[i+1] for i in range(1,len(parts),2)}
files=sorted([p for p in (root/'data').rglob('*.csv') if '/cache/' not in '/'+p.relative_to(root).as_posix()]) + [root/'r2_objects.csv']
for p in files:
    rel=p.relative_to(root).as_posix()
    with p.open('r',encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
        rd=csv.reader(f); hdr=next(rd); rows=list(rd)
    widths=Counter(map(len,rows))
    miss=[sum(1 for r in rows if i>=len(r) or r[i].strip()=='') for i in range(len(hdr))]
    if rel=='r2_objects.csv': keys=['bucket','object_key']
    elif rel in ('data/cme_swap_data.csv','data/treasury_futures_data.csv'): keys=['date','ticker']
    else: keys=['date']
    idx=[hdr.index(k) for k in keys]
    kc=Counter(tuple(r[i] for i in idx) for r in rows)
    dup_extra=sum(c-1 for c in kc.values() if c>1)
    dup_involved=sum(c for c in kc.values() if c>1)
    body=doc[rel]
    drows=int(re.search(r'(?m)^- Rows: (\d+)$',body).group(1))
    dcols=int(re.search(r'(?m)^- Columns: (\d+)$',body).group(1))
    dhdr=re.search(r'(?m)^- Header: `(.*)`$',body).group(1)
    dmraw=re.search(r'(?m)^- Missing counts: (.*)$',body).group(1)
    dm=[int(x.rsplit('=',1)[1]) for x in dmraw.split('; ')]
    print(f'PROFILE\t{rel}\trows={len(rows)}\twidth={len(hdr)}\trow_widths={dict(sorted(widths.items()))}\tdup_headers={len(hdr)-len(set(hdr))}\tkey={"+".join(keys)}\tdup_extra={dup_extra}\tdup_involved={dup_involved}\trows_doc_match={len(rows)==drows}\twidth_doc_match={len(hdr)==dcols}\theader_doc_match={",".join(hdr)==dhdr}\tmissing_doc_match={miss==dm}')
'@ | & '..\p01\.venv\Scripts\python.exe' -
```

```powershell
@'
from pathlib import Path
import csv,re
root=Path(r'C:\Users\jaydo_0v7vk2o\Downloads\Swap_Arbitrage')
text=Path('docs/data/current-inventory.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
parts=re.split(r'(?m)^### `([^`]+)`\r?\n',text)
profiles={parts[i]:parts[i+1] for i in range(1,len(parts),2)}
actual={p.relative_to(root).as_posix() for p in (root/'data').rglob('*.csv')}
if (root/'r2_objects.csv').is_file(): actual.add('r2_objects.csv')
cache=[p for p in actual if p.startswith('data/cache/')]
header_mismatch=[]; width_mismatch=[]
for rel in cache:
    with (root/rel).open('r',encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f: hdr=next(csv.reader(f))
    body=profiles[rel]
    dh=re.search(r'(?m)^- Header: `(.*)`$',body).group(1)
    dc=int(re.search(r'(?m)^- Columns: (\d+)$',body).group(1))
    if ','.join(hdr)!=dh: header_mismatch.append(rel)
    if len(hdr)!=dc: width_mismatch.append(rel)
print('DOC_PROFILE_COUNT',len(profiles))
print('ACTUAL_PATH_COUNT',len(actual))
print('DOC_ONLY',len(set(profiles)-actual))
print('ACTUAL_ONLY',len(actual-set(profiles)))
print('CACHE_HEADER_MISMATCHES',len(header_mismatch))
print('CACHE_WIDTH_MISMATCHES',len(width_mismatch))
'@ | & '..\p01\.venv\Scripts\python.exe' -
```

Findings:

- Exact path sets match: 1,486 recursive data CSVs plus one R2 manifest; 1,474 cache plus 13 current artifacts.
- Every one of 1,487 document profiles matches an actual path; no extra or missing paths.
- All 13 current and all 1,474 cache hashes match. Cache aggregate: `c30dc994872ef415970d1f27425a22f95f9718d18c7f83c1808e73f3db259725`.
- All documented widths, headers, per-column missingness, and known candidate-key duplicate counts match.
- Cache has two 64-column schema variants: 398 files through 2022-04-01 and 1,076 from 2022-04-04.
- Observation: four backtests plus `swap_rates.csv`, `treasury_futures.csv`, and `treasury_rates.csv` have unique `date` values but their key rule remains unknown.

Disposition: accept all quantitative evidence. Preserve the seven key declarations as unknown because observed uniqueness alone is not an authoritative key contract.
